# Harbor Ledger Transparency Incident Transcript

Compiled narrative from bridge chat, council minutes, and operator handoffs regarding the March 2026 key-ceremony migration for the CSV transparency ledger.

## How to read this dossier
Earlier sections may contradict later council rulings. When instructions conflict, the March 12 council addendum at `/app/docs/ceremony_minutes_addendum.md` and the machine readable notice at `/app/data/key_rotation_notice.json` supersede informal bridge notes.

## Archived January draft (superseded)
Draft 0.9 told engineers to join CSV columns with commas for signing because the export tool emitted commas. That draft never shipped and was explicitly revoked during the February 18 dry run. Do not use comma-separated signing payloads.

Bridge note 0001 (00:00Z, museum-east loading dock): Nadia filed a follow-up ticket after seeing operators quote January comma-join notes during badge reader lag checks at museum-east loading dock. The bridge revisited signing payload and captured this ratified wording: Council confirmed signing payloads use pipe separators in this order: seq, tenant, amount_cents, memo, posted_at, signer. The CSV file keeps commas for storage but verification must rebuild the pipe form. Unrelated badge reader lag noise was deferred so verification engineers could focus on the ceremony fix pack.
Bridge note 0002 (01:13Z, west wing security kiosk): Marcus queued a dry-run replay after seeing operators quote January comma-join notes during docent radio chatter checks at west wing security kiosk. The bridge revisited memo normalization and captured this ratified wording: Empty memo cells become the literal string (empty) after trim. Internal whitespace collapses to a single space and memos are NFC-normalized before signing. Unrelated docent radio chatter noise was deferred so verification engineers could focus on the ceremony fix pack.
Bridge note 0003 (02:26Z, after-hours bridge table): Theo paged the trust liaison after seeing operators quote January comma-join notes during crate humidity alarms checks at after-hours bridge table. The bridge revisited timestamp normalization and captured this ratified wording: posted_at values are converted to UTC and rendered with a trailing Z suffix regardless of how the CSV stores offsets. Unrelated crate humidity alarms noise was deferred so verification engineers could focus on the ceremony fix pack.
Bridge note 0004 (03:39Z, ledger export bay): Jonah drafted a rollback memo after seeing operators quote January comma-join notes during printer ribbon drift checks at ledger export bay. The bridge revisited amount canonicalization and captured this ratified wording: amount_cents is treated as a base-10 integer without leading zeros except the lone zero case. Unrelated printer ribbon drift noise was deferred so verification engineers could focus on the ceremony fix pack.
Bridge note 0005 (04:52Z, museum-east loading dock): Cal annotated the bridge timeline after seeing operators quote January comma-join notes during fire panel test window checks at museum-east loading dock. The bridge revisited key rotation and captured this ratified wording: ledger-key-v2 is primary for rows posted on or after 2026-03-01T00:00:00Z. ledger-key-v1 remains valid only for earlier posted_at timestamps. Unrelated fire panel test window noise was deferred so verification engineers could focus on the ceremony fix pack.
Bridge note 0006 (05:05Z, west wing security kiosk): Sofia opened a scratch log after seeing operators quote January comma-join notes during elevator maintenance hold checks at west wing security kiosk. The bridge revisited bootstrap exception and captured this ratified wording: Rows signed by legacy-bootstrap use HMAC-SHA256 with the ceremony seed at /app/data/ceremony_seed.bin through 2026-03-15T00:00:00Z instead of Ed25519. Unrelated elevator maintenance hold noise was deferred so verification engineers could focus on the ceremony fix pack.
Bridge note 0007 (06:18Z, after-hours bridge table): Elena saved a redacted screenshot after seeing operators quote January comma-join notes during camera clock skew checks at after-hours bridge table. The bridge revisited hash chain and captured this ratified wording: Chain state starts from ledger-genesis-v3. Each step hashes prev_digest|row_digest where row_digest is sha256(canonical|signature_hex). Unrelated camera clock skew noise was deferred so verification engineers could focus on the ceremony fix pack.
Bridge note 0008 (07:31Z, ledger export bay): Priya called the council scribe after seeing operators quote January comma-join notes during parking gate firmware checks at ledger export bay. The bridge revisited receipt desk and captured this ratified wording: Customer-facing receipt ids use prefix rcpt-HBR- with a four-digit zero padded sequence number. Unrelated parking gate firmware noise was deferred so verification engineers could focus on the ceremony fix pack.
